CONVERSION_NOTES.md Step 5)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nDATA_DIR = '/app/data'\nCACHE_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'cache')\nONTOLOGY_PATH = os.path.join(CACHE_DIR, 'allen_structure_graph.json')\n\n# Trial window relative to the go cue and bin size (mandated by the Decoder Task).\nOFF_START = -2.5          # s, signed time from go cue to start of the extracted trial\nOFF_END = 1.5             # s, signed time from go cue to end of the extracted trial\nBIN_SIZE = 0.05           # s, 50 ms bins\nN_BINS = int(round((OFF_END - OFF_START) / BIN_SIZE))            # 80\nBIN_EDGES = OFF_START + BIN_SIZE * np.arange(N_BINS + 1)         # (81,)\nBIN_CENTERS = OFF_START + BIN_SIZE * (np.arange(N_BINS) + 0.5)   # (80,)\n\n# Session inclusion criteria from the data paper (\"Behavior and video tracking\"):\n#   overall behavioral performance (> 65%) and at least 50 correct lick left and\n#   lick right trials each.\nMIN_PERFORMANCE = 0.65\nMIN_CORRECT_PER_DIRECTION = 50\n\n# The decoder needs at least two trials per session to be able to train and validate.\nMIN_TRIALS_PER_SESSION = 2\n\n# DeepLabCut likelihood above which the tongue counts as visible.  The likelihood is\n# strongly bimodal (~10.5% of frames > 0.9, the rest < 1e-3), so the exact value is\n# immaterial; 0.9 is the DeepLabCut default cut-off.\nTONGUE_LIKELIHOOD_THRESHOLD = 0.9\n\n# Tongue y-position percentile boundaries (Decoder Task).\nTONGUE_PCTL_LOW = 40.0\nTONGUE_PCTL_HIGH = 60.0\n\n# CCF anterior-posterior coordinate (um) anterior to which a cortical unit is called ALM.\n# Bregma is at CCF (ML, DV, AP) = (5700, 0, 5400) um (cf. reference\n# population_decoding_utils.get_ventral_medial_mask), and AP increases posteriorly, so\n# \"2.0 mm anterior to bregma\" is z < 3400 um.  Reproduces the paper's 8717 ALM units.\nBREGMA_CCF = np.array([5700.0, 0.0, 5400.0])   # (ML, DV, AP)\nALM_AP_MIN_MM = 2.0\n\n# Coarse brain areas of datapaper Fig. 1J, matched against the Allen CCF structure graph\n# by ancestor acronym.  Order matters: the first matching ancestor wins.\nCOARSE_GROUPS = [\n    ('Medulla', 'MY'),\n    ('Pons', 'P'),\n    ('Midbrain', 'MB'),\n    ('Cerebellum', 'CB'),\n    ('Thalamus', 'TH'),\n    ('Hypothalamus', 'HY'),\n    ('Striatum', 'STR'),\n    ('Pallidum', 'PAL'),\n    ('Hippocampus', 'HPF'),\n    ('Olfactory', 'OLF'),\n    ('CorticalSubplate', 'CTXsp'),\n    ('Orbital', 'ORB'),\n    ('OtherCortex', 'Isocortex'),\n]\nBRAIN_REGIONS = ['ALM'] + [name for name, _ in COARSE_GROUPS] + ['Other']\n\nINPUT_NAMES = ['time_from_tone_onset', 'photostim']\nOUTPUT_NAMES = ['choice', 'outcome', 'early_lick', 'tongue_y']\nOUTPUT_VALUES = [\n    ['left', 'right', 'no lick'],\n    ['ignore', 'miss', 'hit'],\n    ['no', 'yes'],\n    ['<40th pctile', '40-60th pctile', '>60th pctile', 'not visible'],\n]\n\nCHOICE_LEFT, CHOICE_RIGHT, CHOICE_NOLICK = 0, 1, 2\nOUTCOME_CODE = {'ignore': 0, 'miss': 1, 'hit': 2}\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Allen CCF ontology -> coarse area\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef load_ontology(path=ONTOLOGY_PATH):\n    \"\"\"Return {structure name: [acronyms from root down to the structure]}.\"\"\"\n    if not os.path.exists(path):\n        raise FileNotFoundError(\n            f'Allen CCF structure graph not found at {path}. Download it with:\\n'\n            \"  curl -o %s 'http://api.brain-map.org/api/v2/structure_graph_download/1.json'\"\n            % path)\n    root = json.load(open(path))['msg'][0]\n    by_name = {}\n\n    def walk(node, acr_path):\n        p = acr_path + [node['acronym']]\n        by_name[node['name']] = p\n        for child in node['children']:\n            walk(child, p)\n\n    walk(root, [])\n    return by_name\n\n\ndef assign_brain_regions(anno_names, ccf_xyz, ontology):\n    \"\"\"Map each unit to an index into BRAIN_REGIONS.\n\n    Args:\n        anno_names: (n_units,) array of Allen CCF structure names.\n        ccf_xyz: (n_units, 3) CCF coordinates (ML, DV, AP) in um.\n        ontology: dict from load_ontology().\n\n    Returns:\n        (n_units,) int array of indices into BRAIN_REGIONS.\n    \"\"\"\n    name_to_idx = {n: i for i, n in enumerate(BRAIN_REGIONS)}\n    ap_mm = (BREGMA_CCF[2] - np.asarray(ccf_xyz)[:, 2]) / 1000.0   # +ve = anterior\n    out = np.full(len(anno_names), name_to_idx['Other'], dtype=np.int64)\n    for i, anno in enumerate(anno_names):\n        path = ontology.get(anno)\n        if path is None:\n            continue\n        group = 'Other'\n        for name, acr in COARSE_GROUPS:\n            if acr in path:\n                group = name\n                break\n        # ALM is carved out of (non-orbital) cortex by its anterior-posterior position.\n        if group == 'OtherCortex' and ap_mm[i] > ALM_AP_MIN_MM:\n            group = 'ALM'\n        out[i] = name_to_idx[group]\n    return out\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# small NWB helpers\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef _dec(arr):\n    \"\"\"Decode an HDF5 object (bytes) column into a numpy array of str.\"\"\"\n    return np.array([x.decode() if isinstance(x, bytes) else str(x) for x in arr])\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Session-level loading\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef read_trial_table(f):\n    \"\"\"Read the fields of intervals/trials that we need, plus the go cue times.\"\"\"\n    t = f['intervals/trials']\n    tr = {\n        'start_time': t['start_time'][:],\n        'stop_time': t['stop_time'][:],\n        'outcome': _dec(t['outcome'][:]),\n        'instruction': _dec(t['trial_instruction'][:]),\n        'early_lick': _dec(t['early_lick'][:]),\n        'auto_water': t['auto_water'][:].astype(int),\n        'free_water': t['free_water'][:].astype(int),\n        'photostim_onset': _dec(t['photostim_onset'][:]),\n        'photostim_duration': _dec(t['photostim_duration'][:]),\n        'photostim_power': _dec(t['photostim_power'][:]),\n    }\n    go = f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/go_start_times']['timestamps'][:]\n    ntrials = len(tr['start_time'])\n    if len(go) != ntrials:\n        raise ValueError('number of go cues (%d) != number of trials (%d)' % (len(go), ntrials))\n    tr['go_time'] = go\n    # Tone (sample epoch) onset: the last sample-epoch start before the go cue.  A trial\n    # whose sample/delay epoch was replayed after an early lick has several; the one that\n    # actually preceded the go cue is the last.\n    sample_start = np.sort(f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/sample_start_times']['timestamps'][:])\n    idx = np.searchsorted(sample_start, go, side='right') - 1\n    tone = np.where(idx >= 0, sample_start[np.maximum(idx, 0)], np.nan)\n    # Fall back to the nominal 0.65 s sample + 1.2 s delay if a trial has no preceding tone.\n    tone = np.where(np.isnan(tone), go - 1.85, tone)\n    tr['tone_time'] = tone\n    return tr\n\n\ndef session_performance(tr):\n    \"\"\"Behavioral performance as defined in the data paper.\n\n    \"Overall performance was computed as the fraction of correct control trials\n    (i.e. no photostimulation), excluding any early lick trials.\"\n    Auto-water / free-water trials are excluded as well since the animal is rewarded\n    regardless of its action.\n    \"\"\"\n    control = ((tr['early_lick'] == 'no early') &\n               (tr['photostim_power'] == 'N/A') &\n               (tr['auto_water'] == 0) &\n               (tr['free_water'] == 0))\n    responded = control & (tr['outcome'] != 'ignore')\n    if responded.sum() == 0:\n        return 0.0\n    return float((tr['outcome'][control] == 'hit').sum() / responded.sum())\n\n\ndef session_passes(f, tr):\n    \"\"\"Data-paper session inclusion criteria, plus the decoder's minimum requirements.\"\"\"\n    perf = session_performance(tr)\n    ncorrect_left = int(((tr['outcome'] == 'hit') & (tr['instruction'] == 'left')).sum())\n    ncorrect_right = int(((tr['outcome'] == 'hit') & (tr['instruction'] == 'right')).sum())\n    ngood = int((_dec(f['units/classification'][:]) == 'good').sum())\n    nkept = int(trial_mask(f, tr).sum())\n\n    reason = ''\n    if perf <= MIN_PERFORMANCE:\n        reason = 'performance <= %.2f' % MIN_PERFORMANCE\n    elif min(ncorrect_left, ncorrect_right) < MIN_CORRECT_PER_DIRECTION:\n        reason = 'fewer than %d correct trials in one direction' % MIN_CORRECT_PER_DIRECTION\n    elif ngood == 0:\n        reason = 'no units passed quality control'\n    elif nkept < MIN_TRIALS_PER_SESSION:\n        reason = 'fewer than %d usable trials' % MIN_TRIALS_PER_SESSION\n    return reason == '', perf, ncorrect_left, ncorrect_right, reason\n\n\ndef observed_trial_mask(f, tr):\n    \"\"\"Trials during which the units were actually being recorded.\n\n    `units/obs_intervals` lists, per unit, the [start, stop] interval of every trial the\n    unit was observed in.  In 9 of the 174 sessions the electrophysiology covers only a\n    contiguous block of the behavioral session (as few as 160 of 480 trials); the\n    remaining trials have no spikes at all and would otherwise be converted into\n    all-zero firing rate matrices.  The intervals are identical for every unit of a\n    session, so we read them for the first and the last unit and check that they agree.\n    \"\"\"\n    u = f['units']\n    index = u['obs_intervals_index'][:].astype(np.int64)\n    first = u['obs_intervals'][0:index[0]]\n    if len(index) > 1:\n        last = u['obs_intervals'][index[-2]:index[-1]]\n        if last.shape != first.shape or not np.allclose(first, last):\n            raise ValueError('obs_intervals differ between units')\n\n    start = tr['start_time']\n    pos = np.searchsorted(start, first[:, 0])\n    pos = np.clip(pos, 0, len(start) - 1)\n    if not (np.allclose(start[pos], first[:, 0]) and\n            np.allclose(tr['stop_time'][pos], first[:, 1])):\n        raise ValueError('obs_intervals do not line up with the trial table')\n    mask = np.zeros(len(start), dtype=bool)\n    mask[pos] = True\n    return mask\n\n\ndef trial_mask(f, tr):\n    \"\"\"Trials kept for conversion.\n\n    All trials are kept except\n      * auto-water and free-water trials, on which water is delivered irrespective of the\n        animal's action so that `outcome` and `choice` do not reflect a decision (the\n        reference `get_regular_trial_mask` excludes them too), and\n      * trials outside the units' observation intervals, which carry no spikes.\n    Early-lick, no-response and photostimulation trials are deliberately kept because the\n    decoder must predict / receive exactly those variables.\n    \"\"\"\n    return ((tr['auto_water'] == 0) & (tr['free_water'] == 0) &\n            observed_trial_mask(f, tr))\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Neural data\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef load_good_units(f, ontology):\n    \"\"\"Return spike time arrays and brain-region indices for the session's good units.\n\n    Neuron curation follows the paper: units labelled 'good' by the region-specific\n    quality-control classifier (units/classification), which also guarantees a CCF\n    annotation (units/anno_name).\n    \"\"\"\n    u = f['units']\n    classification = _dec(u['classification'][:])\n    good = np.flatnonzero(classification == 'good')\n\n    anno = _dec(u['anno_name'][:])[good]\n    e = f['general/extracellular_ephys/electrodes']\n    exyz = np.stack([e['x'][:], e['y'][:], e['z'][:]], axis=1)\n    eidx = u['electrodes'][:][good]\n    ccf_xyz = exyz[eidx]\n\n    region_idx = assign_brain_regions(anno, ccf_xyz, ontology)\n\n    spike_times = u['spike_times'][:]\n    spike_index = u['spike_times_index'][:]\n    starts = np.concatenate([[0], spike_index[:-1]]).astype(np.int64)\n    ends = spike_index.astype(np.int64)\n    spikes = [spike_times[starts[i]:ends[i]] for i in good]\n\n    return spikes, region_idx, anno, ccf_xyz\n\n\ndef bin_spikes(spikes, go_times):\n    \"\"\"Bin spikes into firing rates aligned to the go cue.\n\n    Args:\n        spikes: list of n_units sorted arrays of absolute spike times (s).\n        go_times: (ntrials,) absolute go cue times (s).\n\n    Returns:\n        (ntrials, n_units, N_BINS) float32 array of firing rates in Hz.\n    \"\"\"\n    ntrials = len(go_times)\n    edges = (go_times[:, None] + BIN_EDGES[None, :]).ravel()    # (ntrials*81,)\n    fr = np.empty((ntrials, len(spikes), N_BINS), dtype=np.float32)\n    for i, st in enumerate(spikes):\n        pos = np.searchsorted(st, edges).reshape(ntrials, N_BINS + 1)\n        fr[:, i, :] = np.diff(pos, axis=1)\n    fr /= BIN_SIZE\n    return fr\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Decoder inputs\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef build_inputs(tr, keep):\n    \"\"\"Build the (ntrials, 2, N_BINS) float32 decoder input array.\n\n    input[0] = time from tone (sample epoch) onset, in seconds, at each bin centre\n    input[1] = 1 while ALM photostimulation is on, 0 otherwise\n    \"\"\"\n    go = tr['go_time'][keep]\n    tone = tr['tone_time'][keep]\n    n = len(go)\n\n    inp = np.zeros((n, 2, N_BINS), dtype=np.float32)\n    # Time since the tone onset at each bin centre (both expressed relative to the go cue).\n    inp[:, 0, :] = (BIN_CENTERS[None, :] - (tone - go)[:, None]).astype(np.float32)\n\n    onset_str = tr['photostim_onset'][keep]\n    dur_str = tr['photostim_duration'][keep]\n    start = tr['start_time'][keep]\n    has_stim = onset_str != 'N/A'\n    if has_stim.any():\n        onset = np.zeros(n)\n        dur = np.zeros(n)\n        onset[has_stim] = np.array([float(x) for x in onset_str[has_stim]])\n        dur[has_stim] = np.array([float(x) for x in dur_str[has_stim]])\n        # photostim_onset is relative to the trial start_time; express relative to go cue.\n        on_rel = start + onset - go\n        off_rel = on_rel + dur\n        inside = ((BIN_CENTERS[None, :] >= on_rel[:, None]) &\n                  (BIN_CENTERS[None, :] < off_rel[:, None]))\n        inp[:, 1, :] = (inside & has_stim[:, None]).astype(np.float32)\n    return inp\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Decoder outputs\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef choice_codes(tr, keep):\n    \"\"\"Lick direction chosen by the animal.\n\n    hit    -> the animal licked the instructed port\n    miss   -> the animal licked the other port\n    ignore -> no lick\n    (Equivalent to the reference's `behavior_report` x `task_trial_type` combination;\n    verified against the first lick after the go cue in Step 10.)\n    \"\"\"\n    outcome = tr['outcome'][keep]\n    instr = tr['instruction'][keep]\n    instr_code = np.where(instr == 'left', CHOICE_LEFT, CHOICE_RIGHT)\n    opposite = np.where(instr == 'left', CHOICE_RIGHT, CHOICE_LEFT)\n    choice = np.where(outcome == 'hit', instr_code,\n                      np.where(outcome == 'miss', opposite, CHOICE_NOLICK))\n    return choice.astype(np.int64)\n\n\ndef bin_tongue(f, go_times):\n    \"\"\"Bin the side-view tongue y-position onto the trial grid.\n\n    Returns:\n        y_bin: (ntrials, N_BINS) float array, mean y over the *visible* frames of the bin\n               (NaN if no visible frame falls in the bin)\n        visible: (ntrials, N_BINS) bool array\n    \"\"\"\n    tt = f['acquisition/BehavioralTimeSeries/Camera0_side_TongueTracking']\n    data = tt['data']\n    ts = tt['timestamps'][:]\n    y = data[:, 1]\n    lik = data[:, 2]\n    vis = lik > TONGUE_LIKELIHOOD_THRESHOLD\n\n    ntrials = len(go_times)\n    y_bin = np.full((ntrials, N_BINS), np.nan)\n    lo = np.searchsorted(ts, go_times + OFF_START)\n    hi = np.searchsorted(ts, go_times + OFF_END)\n    for i in range(ntrials):\n        sl = slice(lo[i], hi[i])\n        if sl.stop <= sl.start:\n            continue\n        rel = ts[sl] - go_times[i]\n        v = vis[sl]\n        if not v.any():\n            continue\n        b = np.floor((rel[v] - OFF_START) / BIN_SIZE).astype(np.int64)\n        np.clip(b, 0, N_BINS - 1, out=b)\n        counts = np.bincount(b, minlength=N_BINS)\n        sums = np.bincount(b, weights=y[sl][v], minlength=N_BINS)\n        nz = counts > 0\n        y_bin[i, nz] = sums[nz] / counts[nz]\n    return y_bin, ~np.isnan(y_bin)\n\n\ndef discretize_tongue(y_bin):\n    \"\"\"Per-session discretisation of the binned tongue y-position (Decoder Task).\n\n    0: y < 40th percentile of the session's visible y values\n    1: 40th - 60th percentile\n    2: y > 60th percentile\n    3: tongue not visible\n    \"\"\"\n    visible = ~np.isnan(y_bin)\n    out = np.full(y_bin.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)\n    if visible.sum() == 0:\n        return out, (np.nan, np.nan)\n    vals = y_bin[visible]\n    p40, p60 = np.percentile(vals, [TONGUE_PCTL_LOW, TONGUE_PCTL_HIGH])\n    code = np.where(y_bin < p40, 0, np.where(y_bin > p60, 2, 1))\n    out[visible] = code[visible]\n    return out, (float(p40), float(p60))\n\n\ndef build_outputs(f, tr, keep):\n    \"\"\"Build the (ntrials, 4, N_BINS) int64 decoder output array.\"\"\"\n    go = tr['go_time'][keep]\n    n = len(go)\n    out = np.empty((n, 4, N_BINS), dtype=np.int64)\n\n    out[:, 0, :] = choice_codes(tr, keep)[:, None]\n    out[:, 1, :] = np.array([OUTCOME_CODE[o] for o in tr['outcome'][keep]])[:, None]\n    out[:, 2, :] = (tr['early_lick'][keep] == 'early').astype(np.int64)[:, None]\n\n    y_bin, _ = bin_tongue(f, go)\n    tongue, pctls = discretize_tongue(y_bin)\n    out[:, 3, :] = tongue\n    return out, y_bin, pctls\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# One session\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef process_session(filepath, show_processing=False, ontology=None):\n    \"\"\"Convert a single NWB session.  Returns None if the session fails the criteria.\"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n    if ontology is None:\n        ontology = load_ontology()\n\n    with h5py.File(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        session_id = f['identifier'][()].decode()\n        subject_id = f['general/subject/subject_id'][()].decode()\n        mouse = session_id.split('_')[0]\n\n        tr = read_trial_table(f)\n        ok, perf, ncl, ncr, reason = session_passes(f, tr)\n        if not ok:\n            return {'session_id': session_id, 'excluded': True, 'performance': perf,\n                    'ncorrect_left': ncl, 'ncorrect_right': ncr, 'reason': reason,\n                    'ntrials_total': len(tr['start_time'])}\n\n        keep = trial_mask(f, tr)\n        t_read = time.time()\n\n        spikes, region_idx, anno, ccf_xyz = load_good_units(f, ontology)\n        t_units = time.time()\n\n        go = tr['go_time'][keep]\n        fr = bin_spikes(spikes, go)\n\n        # A 4 s window in which not one of several hundred simultaneously recorded\n        # neurons fires is not biologically possible; it means the window is outside the\n        # ephys recording (the last trial listed in obs_intervals can extend past the end\n        # of the recording).  Drop those trials.\n        silent = fr.sum(axis=(1, 2)) == 0\n        n_silent = int(silent.sum())\n        if n_silent:\n            kept_positions = np.flatnonzero(keep)\n            keep[kept_positions[silent]] = False\n            fr = fr[~silent]\n        t_bin = time.time()\n\n        inp = build_inputs(tr, keep)\n        out, y_bin, pctls = build_outputs(f, tr, keep)\n        t_io = time.time()\n\n        result = {\n            'session_id': session_id,\n            'excluded': False,\n            'subject': mouse,\n            'subject_id': subject_id,\n            'neural': [np.ascontiguousarray(fr[i]) for i in range(fr.shape[0])],\n            'input': [np.ascontiguousarray(inp[i]) for i in range(inp.shape[0])],\n            'output': [np.ascontiguousarray(out[i]) for i in range(out.shape[0])],\n            'brain_region_idx': region_idx,\n            'performance': perf,\n            'ncorrect_left': ncl,\n            'ncorrect_right': ncr,\n            'ntrials_total': int(len(tr['start_time'])),\n            'ntrials_kept': int(keep.sum()),\n            'nneurons': int(len(spikes)),\n            'tongue_percentiles': pctls,\n            # diagnostics: number of trials dropped because no neuron fired in them\n            'n_silent_trials': n_silent,\n            'mean_rate_hz': float(fr.mean()),\n            'timing':{'read': t_read - t0, 'units': t_units - t_read,\n                       'bin': t_bin - t_units, 'io': t_io - t_bin,\n                       'total': t_io - t0},\n        }\n\n        if show_processing:\n            plot_processing(f, tr, keep, spikes, fr, inp, out, y_bin, pctls,\n                            region_idx, session_id)\n\n    return result\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Diagnostic plots\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef plot_processing(f, tr, keep, spikes, fr, inp, out, y_bin, pctls, region_idx,\n                    session_id):\n    \"\"\"Plot every processing step for one session so that it can be checked visually.\"\"\"\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    go_all = tr['go_time']\n    go = go_all[keep]\n    ntrials = len(go)\n    kept_idx = np.flatnonzero(keep)\n\n    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(22, 26))\n    gs = fig.add_gridspec(7, 3, hspace=0.45, wspace=0.25)\n\n    # ---- 1. raw spike raster vs binned rate, for one example trial -------------------\n    # pick a trial with a lick and a neuron with a decent rate\n    rate_mean = fr.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n    ineuron = int(np.argmax(rate_mean))\n    # choose a trial with a well-tracked tongue after the go cue\n    vis_after = (out[:, 3, N_BINS // 2:] < 3).sum(axis=1)\n    itrial = int(np.argmax(vis_after))\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, :])\n    st = spikes[ineuron]\n    rel = st[(st >= go[itrial] + OFF_START) & (st < go[itrial] + OFF_END)] - go[itrial]\n    ax.eventplot(rel, colors='k', lineoffsets=1.0, linelengths=0.8)\n    ax2 = ax.twinx()\n    ax2.step(BIN_CENTERS, fr[itrial, ineuron], where='mid', color='tab:red')\n    ax2.set_ylabel('binned rate (Hz)', color='tab:red')\n    for e in BIN_EDGES[::4]:\n        ax.axvline(e, color='0.9', lw=0.5, zorder=0)\n    ax.axvline(0, color='b', lw=1.5)\n    ax.set_xlim(OFF_START, OFF_END)\n    ax.set_title(f'{session_id}: STEP 1 raw spike times (black ticks) vs 50 ms binned '\n                 f'firing rate (red), neuron {ineuron}, trial {itrial}; '\n                 f'blue line = go cue (t=0)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n\n    # ---- 2. spike count check for that trial ----------------------------------------\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 0])\n    counts_direct = np.histogram(rel, bins=BIN_EDGES)[0] / BIN_SIZE\n    ax.plot(BIN_CENTERS, counts_direct, 'k-', lw=3, label='np.histogram of raw times')\n    ax.plot(BIN_CENTERS, fr[itrial, ineuron], 'r--', lw=1.5, label='converted')\n    ax.legend(fontsize=8)\n    ax.set_title('STEP 1 check: independent re-binning matches\\n'\n                 f'max |diff| = {np.abs(counts_direct - fr[itrial, ineuron]).max():.3g}')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('rate (Hz)')\n\n    # ---- 3. population PSTH aligned to go cue ---------------------------------------\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 1])\n    psth = fr.mean(axis=(0, 1))\n    ax.plot(BIN_CENTERS, psth, 'k')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='b')\n    ax.axvline(np.median(tr['tone_time'][keep] - go), color='g', ls='--')\n    ax.set_title('STEP 2: population PSTH (all neurons, all trials)\\n'\n                 'blue = go cue, green = median tone onset')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('mean rate (Hz)')\n\n    # ---- 4. neuron x time heatmap of trial-averaged rates ---------------------------\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 2])\n    m = fr.mean(axis=0)\n    z = (m - m.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)) / (m.std(axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9)\n    order = np.argsort(np.argmax(z, axis=1))\n    im = ax.imshow(z[order], aspect='auto', vmin=-3, vmax=3, cmap='RdBu_r',\n                   extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, len(order), 0])\n    ax.axvline(0, color='k')\n    ax.set_title('STEP 2: z-scored trial-averaged rate\\n(neurons sorted by peak time)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('neuron')\n    plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax)\n\n    # ---- 5. inputs -------------------------------------------------------------------\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, 0])\n    for i in range(min(8, ntrials)):\n        ax.plot(BIN_CENTERS, inp[i, 0], alpha=0.7)\n    ax.axvline(0, color='b')\n    ax.axhline(0, color='k', lw=0.5)\n    ax.set_title('STEP 3 input[0]: time from tone onset (s)\\n'\n                 '0-crossing should be near t = -1.85 s')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, 1])\n    stim_trials = np.flatnonzero(inp[:, 1].any(axis=1))\n    im = ax.imshow(inp[:, 1], aspect='auto', cmap='Greys',\n                   extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, ntrials, 0], interpolation='nearest')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='b')\n    ax.set_title('STEP 3 input[1]: photostim on (%d/%d trials)\\n'\n                 'must always end at or before the go cue' % (len(stim_trials), ntrials))\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('trial')\n\n    # cross-check against the raw photostim event times\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, 2])\n    ev_on = f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/photostim_start_times']['timestamps'][:]\n    ev_off = f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/photostim_stop_times']['timestamps'][:]\n    if len(ev_on):\n        # assign each raw photostim event to the trial whose [start, stop] contains it\n        j = np.clip(np.searchsorted(tr['start_time'], ev_on, side='right') - 1,\n                    0, len(go_all) - 1)\n        okj = (ev_on >= tr['start_time'][j]) & (ev_on <= tr['stop_time'][j])\n        ax.hist(ev_on[okj] - go_all[j[okj]], bins=40, alpha=0.6, label='raw event onset')\n        derived = [BIN_EDGES[np.flatnonzero(inp[i, 1] > 0)[0]] for i in stim_trials]\n        if derived:\n            ax.hist(derived, bins=40, alpha=0.6, label='converted bin onset')\n        ax.legend(fontsize=8)\n    ax.set_title('STEP 3 check: photostim onset re. go cue\\n(raw events vs converted)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n\n    # ---- 6. tongue tracking ----------------------------------------------------------\n    tt = f['acquisition/BehavioralTimeSeries/Camera0_side_TongueTracking']\n    ts = tt['timestamps'][:]\n    ydat = tt['data'][:, 1]\n    lik = tt['data'][:, 2]\n    p40, p60 = pctls\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[3, :])\n    lo, hi = np.searchsorted(ts, [go[itrial] + OFF_START, go[itrial] + OFF_END])\n    relt = ts[lo:hi] - go[itrial]\n    visraw = lik[lo:hi] > TONGUE_LIKELIHOOD_THRESHOLD\n    ax.plot(relt[~visraw], ydat[lo:hi][~visraw], '.', ms=2, color='0.8',\n            label='raw y (not visible)')\n    ax.plot(relt[visraw], ydat[lo:hi][visraw], '.', ms=3, color='tab:blue',\n            label='raw y (visible)')\n    ax.plot(BIN_CENTERS, y_bin[itrial], 'o-', color='tab:orange', ms=4,\n            label='binned mean y (visible frames)')\n    ax.axhline(p40, color='g', ls='--', label='40th pctile')\n    ax.axhline(p60, color='r', ls='--', label='60th pctile')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='b')\n    for b in range(N_BINS):\n        ax.text(BIN_CENTERS[b], ax.get_ylim()[0], str(out[itrial, 3, b]),\n                fontsize=5, ha='center', va='bottom')\n    ax.set_xlim(OFF_START, OFF_END)\n    ax.legend(fontsize=8, loc='upper left')\n    ax.set_title(f'STEP 4: tongue y tracking -> discretised class (digits at the bottom), '\n                 f'trial {itrial}. Classes: 0 = <p40, 1 = p40-p60, 2 = >p60, 3 = not visible')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('tongue y (px)')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[4, 0])\n    vals = y_bin[~np.isnan(y_bin)]\n    ax.hist(vals, bins=60, color='0.6')\n    ax.axvline(p40, color='g', ls='--')\n    ax.axvline(p60, color='r', ls='--')\n    frac = [np.mean(out[:, 3] == k) for k in range(4)]\n    ax.set_title('STEP 4: session distribution of visible binned y\\n'\n                 'class fractions (incl. class 3): %s' %\n                 np.array2string(np.array(frac), precision=3))\n    ax.set_xlabel('tongue y (px)')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[4, 1])\n    im = ax.imshow(out[:, 3], aspect='auto', cmap='viridis', vmin=0, vmax=3,\n                   extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, ntrials, 0], interpolation='nearest')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='w')\n    ax.set_title('STEP 4: tongue class per trial x bin\\n(class 3 = not visible, yellow)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('trial')\n    plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax)\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[4, 2])\n    ax.plot(BIN_CENTERS, (out[:, 3] < 3).mean(axis=0), 'k')\n    # lick rate for comparison\n    L = f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/left_lick_times']['timestamps'][:]\n    R = f['acquisition/BehavioralEvents/right_lick_times']['timestamps'][:]\n    allL = np.sort(np.concatenate([L, R]))\n    lick = np.zeros(N_BINS)\n    for g in go:\n        a, b = np.searchsorted(allL, [g + OFF_START, g + OFF_END])\n        if b > a:\n            idx = np.clip(((allL[a:b] - g - OFF_START) / BIN_SIZE).astype(int), 0, N_BINS - 1)\n            np.add.at(lick, idx, 1)\n    ax2 = ax.twinx()\n    ax2.plot(BIN_CENTERS, lick / ntrials / BIN_SIZE, color='tab:red')\n    ax2.set_ylabel('lick rate (Hz)', color='tab:red')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='b')\n    ax.set_title('STEP 4 check: P(tongue visible) (black) vs lick rate (red)\\n'\n                 'both must rise right after the go cue')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n\n    # ---- 7. per-trial outputs --------------------------------------------------------\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[5, 0])\n    for k, nm in enumerate(OUTPUT_NAMES[:3]):\n        vals, cnts = np.unique(out[:, k, 0], return_counts=True)\n        ax.bar(np.array(vals) + 4 * k, cnts / ntrials, width=0.8,\n               label=nm)\n    ax.legend(fontsize=8)\n    ax.set_xticks([0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 6, 8, 9])\n    ax.set_xticklabels(OUTPUT_VALUES[0] + OUTPUT_VALUES[1] + OUTPUT_VALUES[2],\n                       rotation=60, fontsize=7)\n    ax.set_title('STEP 5: per-trial output class fractions')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[5, 1])\n    # choice cross-check with the first lick after the go cue\n    derived, observed = [], []\n    for i, g in enumerate(go):\n        a, b = np.searchsorted(allL, [g, g + 3.0])\n        l = L[(L >= g) & (L < g + 3.0)]\n        r = R[(R >= g) & (R < g + 3.0)]\n        if len(l) == 0 and len(r) == 0:\n            o = CHOICE_NOLICK\n        elif len(r) == 0 or (len(l) > 0 and l[0] < r[0]):\n            o = CHOICE_LEFT\n        else:\n            o = CHOICE_RIGHT\n        observed.append(o)\n        derived.append(out[i, 0, 0])\n    derived = np.array(derived); observed = np.array(observed)\n    cm = np.zeros((3, 3))\n    for a_, b_ in zip(derived, observed):\n        cm[a_, b_] += 1\n    im = ax.imshow(cm, cmap='Blues')\n    for a_ in range(3):\n        for b_ in range(3):\n            ax.text(b_, a_, int(cm[a_, b_]), ha='center', va='center', fontsize=8)\n    ax.set_xticks(range(3)); ax.set_xticklabels(OUTPUT_VALUES[0], fontsize=7)\n    ax.set_yticks(range(3)); ax.set_yticklabels(OUTPUT_VALUES[0], fontsize=7)\n    ax.set_xlabel('observed (first lick after go cue)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('converted choice')\n    ax.set_title('STEP 5 check: choice vs licks\\nagreement = %.4f' %\n                 float((derived == observed).mean()))\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[5, 2])\n    counts = np.bincount(region_idx, minlength=len(BRAIN_REGIONS))\n    nz = counts > 0\n    ax.bar(np.arange(nz.sum()), counts[nz])\n    ax.set_xticks(np.arange(nz.sum()))\n    ax.set_xticklabels(np.array(BRAIN_REGIONS)[nz], rotation=60, fontsize=7)\n    ax.set_title('STEP 6: good units per brain region (n = %d)' % len(region_idx))\n\n    # ---- 8. final arrays for a few trials -------------------------------------------\n    for j, itr in enumerate(np.linspace(0, ntrials - 1, 3).astype(int)):\n        ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[6, j])\n        ax.imshow(fr[itr], aspect='auto', cmap='magma',\n                  extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, fr.shape[1], 0],\n                  vmax=np.percentile(fr[itr], 99.5) + 1e-6, interpolation='nearest')\n        ax.axvline(0, color='c')\n        ax.set_title('FINAL neural[%d] (%d x %d)\\nchoice=%s outcome=%s early=%s' %\n                     (itr, fr.shape[1], N_BINS,\n                      OUTPUT_VALUES[0][out[itr, 0, 0]],\n                      OUTPUT_VALUES[1][out[itr, 1, 0]],\n                      OUTPUT_VALUES[2][out[itr, 2, 0]]), fontsize=9)\n        ax.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n        if j == 0:\n            ax.set_ylabel('neuron')\n\n    fig.suptitle('Processing steps for session %s' % session_id, fontsize=14)\n    fname = 'processing_%s.png' % session_id\n    fig.savefig(fname, dpi=110, bbox_inches='tight')\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print('  wrote %s' % fname, flush=True)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Driver\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n_WORKER_ONTOLOGY = None\n\n\ndef _worker_init():\n    global _WORKER_ONTOLOGY\n    _WORKER_ONTOLOGY = load_ontology()\n\n\ndef _worker(args):\n    filepath, show = args\n    try:\n        return process_session(filepath, show_processing=show,\n                               ontology=_WORKER_ONTOLOGY)\n    except Exception as exc:                      # pragma: no cover - defensive\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return {'session_id': os.path.basename(filepath), 'excluded': True,\n                'error': str(exc)}\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    ap.add_argument('outfile', type=str)\n    g = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group()\n    g.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True)\n    g.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true')\n    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true')\n    ap.add_argument('--njobs', type=int, default=min(16, os.cpu_count() or 1))\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    t_start = time.time()\n    files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, 'sub-*', '*.nwb')))\n    print('Found %d NWB files' % len(files), flush=True)\n\n    ontology = load_ontology()\n\n    # --- pass 1: session selection (cheap: only the trials table is read) -------------\n    selected = []\n    excluded = []\n    for fp in files:\n        with h5py.File(fp, 'r') as f:\n            sid = f['identifier'][()].decode()\n            tr = read_trial_table(f)\n            ok, perf, ncl, ncr, reason = session_passes(f, tr)\n        if ok:\n            selected.append(fp)\n        else:\n            excluded.append((sid, perf, ncl, ncr, reason))\n    print('Session selection (performance > %.2f and >= %d correct per direction):'\n          % (MIN_PERFORMANCE, MIN_CORRECT_PER_DIRECTION), flush=True)\n    print('  selected: %d, excluded: %d' % (len(selected), len(excluded)), flush=True)\n    for sid, perf, ncl, ncr, reason in excluded:\n        print('    excluded %s: performance=%.3f correct L/R=%d/%d  [%s]'\n              % (sid, perf, ncl, ncr, reason), flush=True)\n    print('  pass 1 took %.1f s' % (time.time() - t_start), flush=True)\n\n    if args.sample:\n        selected = selected[:2]\n        print('--sample: restricting to %d sessions' % len(selected), flush=True)\n\n    nshow = 2 if args.show_processing else 0\n    jobs = [(fp, i < nshow) for i, fp in enumerate(selected)]\n\n    # --- pass 2: full conversion ------------------------------------------------------\n    results = []\n    t_convert = time.time()\n    if args.njobs > 1 and len(jobs) > 1:\n        import multiprocessing as mp\n        ctx = mp.get_context('spawn')\n        with ctx.Pool(args.njobs, initializer=_worker_init) as pool:\n            for i, res in enumerate(pool.imap(_worker, jobs)):\n                results.append(res)\n                el = time.time() - t_convert\n                print('[%3d/%3d] %s  ntrials=%d/%d nneurons=%d rate=%.2fHz silent=%d '\n                      '(%.1fs; elapsed %.1fs, eta %.1fs)'\n                      % (i + 1, len(jobs), res['session_id'], res.get('ntrials_kept', 0),\n                         res.get('ntrials_total', 0), res.get('nneurons', 0),\n                         res.get('mean_rate_hz', 0), res.get('n_silent_trials', 0),\n                         res.get('timing', {}).get('total', 0),\n                         el, el / (i + 1) * (len(jobs) - i - 1)), flush=True)\n    else:\n        for i, job in enumerate(jobs):\n            res = process_session(job[0], show_processing=job[1], ontology=ontology)\n            results.append(res)\n            el = time.time() - t_convert\n            print('[%3d/%3d] %s  ntrials=%d/%d nneurons=%d rate=%.2fHz silent=%d '\n                  '(%.1fs; elapsed %.1fs)'\n                  % (i + 1, len(jobs), res['session_id'], res.get('ntrials_kept', 0),\n                     res.get('ntrials_total', 0), res.get('nneurons', 0),\n                     res.get('mean_rate_hz', 0), res.get('n_silent_trials', 0),\n                     res.get('timing', {}).get('total', 0), el), flush=True)\n\n    results = [r for r in results if not r.get('excluded', False)]\n    dropped = [r for r in results\n               if len(r['neural']) < MIN_TRIALS_PER_SESSION or r['nneurons'] == 0]\n    for r in dropped:\n        print('  dropping %s after conversion: %d trials, %d neurons'\n              % (r['session_id'], len(r['neural']), r['nneurons']), flush=True)\n    results = [r for r in results\n               if len(r['neural']) >= MIN_TRIALS_PER_SESSION and r['nneurons'] > 0]\n    results.sort(key=lambda r: r['session_id'])\n    print('Converted %d sessions in %.1f s' % (len(results), time.time() - t_convert),\n          flush=True)\n\n    # --- assemble -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n    sub_to_idx = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n    # the mouse name and the DANDI subject id must be in one-to-one correspondence\n    pairs = {(r['subject'], r['subject_id']) for r in results}\n    assert len(pairs) == len(subjects), 'mouse name / subject id mapping is not 1:1'\n\n    data = {\n        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_to_idx[r['subject']] for r in results], dtype=np.int64),\n        'brain_regions': list(BRAIN_REGIONS),\n        'brain_region_idx': [r['brain_region_idx'] for r in results],\n        'input_names': list(INPUT_NAMES),\n        'output_names': list(OUTPUT_NAMES),\n        'output_values': [list(v) for v in OUTPUT_VALUES],\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description':\n                'Mice performed an auditory delayed-response (memory-guided directional '\n                'licking) task. A series of three pure tones (3 kHz -> lick right, '\n                '12 kHz -> lick left; 0.65 s sample epoch) instructed the upcoming lick '\n                'direction; after a 1.2 s delay epoch an auditory go cue (6 kHz, 0.1 s) '\n                'released the animal to lick one of two lick ports during a 1.5 s answer '\n                'period. Licking the correct port gave a water reward. Licking during the '\n                'sample/delay epoch (\"early lick\") triggered a replay of the epoch. On '\n                '~20% of randomly interleaved trials, ALM was bilaterally or unilaterally '\n                'photoinhibited (5.5 mW, 0.5 s, always ending at or before the go cue). '\n                'Decoder outputs are the lick direction chosen (left / right / no lick), '\n                'the trial outcome (ignore / miss / hit), whether the trial contained an '\n                'early lick (no / yes), and the discretised side-view tongue y-position '\n                '(below 40th pctile / 40-60th pctile / above 60th pctile / not visible). '\n                'Decoder inputs are the time since the instruction-tone onset and a binary '\n                'photostimulation indicator.',\n            'time_bin_size': BIN_SIZE * 1000.0,            # ms\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'go cue onset (auditory go cue, 6 kHz, 0.1 s), '\n                                        'NWB acquisition/BehavioralEvents/go_start_times',\n            'off_start': OFF_START,\n            'off_end': OFF_END,\n            'bin_centers': BIN_CENTERS.copy(),             # s, relative to the go cue\n            'neural_units': 'firing rate (spikes/s), spike counts in 50 ms '\n                            'non-overlapping bins divided by the bin width',\n            'input_units': ['s', 'binary (1 = photostimulation on)'],\n            'dataset': 'DANDI:000363 Mesoscale Activity Map Dataset '\n                       '(Chen, Nguyen, Li & Svoboda 2023), doi:10.48324/dandi.000363',\n            'references': [\n                'Chen et al., Brain-wide neural activity underlying memory-guided movement',\n                'Wang, Kurgyis et al., Brain-wide analysis reveals movement encoding '\n                'structured across and within brain areas (Nat Neurosci 2025)',\n            ],\n            'neuron_curation': \"units/classification == 'good' (region-specific \"\n                               'logistic-regression quality-control classifier of the '\n                               'Chen/Liu et al. 2023 spike-sorting white paper)',\n            'trial_curation': 'all trials except auto-water and free-water trials',\n            'session_curation':\n                'behavioral performance on control (no photostim, no early lick, no '\n                'auto/free water) trials > 65%% and at least 50 `go_start_times` in NWB). The reference marker alignment also uses the go cue with a window of\n   141→  −3 … +1.5 s. Our task window is −2.5 … +1.5 s.\n   142→- **Temporal binning**: reference 40 ms / 3.4 ms sliding; the Decoder Task here mandates **50 ms\n   143→  bins** → 80 non-overlapping bins over the 4 s window. Licking during sample/delay triggers a *replay* of the epoch, so a trial can have more\n   147→  than one `sample_start_times` / `delay_start_times` event (5.8 % of trials).\n   148→\n   149→### Curation Steps\n   150→\n   151→**Neuron curation rules (reference)**:\n   152→1. (Method paper, analysis-level only) brain areas with < 10 neurons in a session were dropped from\n   156→   *per-area* analyses; not applicable here because we pool all neurons of a session.\n See Step 5 decision 4. |\n   209→| Tongue occlusion | – | DLC likelihood is strongly bimodal (10.5 % of frames > 0.9, ~89.5 % < 1e-3) | \"set the tongue position to its mean value\" when occluded | The Decoder Task instead defines an explicit 4th class \"not visible\", which supersedes the reference's imputation. Threshold 0.9 (any threshold in 1e-3 … 0.99 changes the visible fraction by < 0.3 %). |\n   210→\n   211→### Consistency check: per-area good-unit counts\n   212→Coarse areas were reconstructed from `units/anno_name` using the **Allen CCF structure graph**\n   213→(downloaded to `cache/allen_structure_graph.json`, mapping code in `cache/region_map.py`):\n   214→a unit belongs to the first matching ancestor among MY, P, MB, CB, TH, HY, STR, PAL, HPF, OLF,\n   215→CTXsp, ORB, Isocortex.\n   216→\n   217→| Area | Paper (Fig. 1J) | This conversion (all 174 sessions) | Match |\n   218→|---|---|---|---|\n   219→| Thalamus | 12,808 | **12,808** | exact |\n   220→| Orbital | 10,223 | **10,223** | exact |\n   221→| Striatum | 7,664 | **7,664** | exact |\n   222→| Midbrain | 7,495 | **7,495** | exact |\n   223→| Olfactory | 4,137 | **4,137** | exact |\n   224→| Medulla | 2,928 | **2,928** | exact |\n   225→| Cortical subplate | 1,960 | **1,960** | exact |\n   226→| Hippocampus | 1,944 | **1,944** | exact |\n   227→| Cerebellum | 1,820 | **1,820** | exact |\n   228→| Pallidum | 1,092 | **1,092** | exact |\n   229→| Hypothalamus | 815 | **815** | exact |\n   230→| Pons | 347 | **347** | exact |\n   231→| ALM + other cortex | 8,717 + 7,993 = 16,710 | 16,220 | −490 (dandiset version) |\n   232→| **Total** | 69,943 | 69,453 | −490 |\n   233→\n   234→This is a decisive confirmation that `classification == 'good'` is the paper's QC criterion and that\n   235→the CCF-ontology grouping reproduces the paper's area definitions.\n   236→\n   237→**ALM**: the reference splits ALM out of cortex with a voxel mask (`ALM_voxels_symmetric.npy`, not\n   238→distributed). Reconstructing it as *cortical units more than 2.0 mm anterior to bregma*\n   239→(CCF AP coordinate `z < 3400 µm`) yields **8,728** units versus the paper's 8,717 (0.1 %\n   240→difference); all such units also fall within |ML| < 2.2 mm of the midline, i.e. the ALM location\n   241→(AP 2.5, ML 1.5) of `methods.txt`. Thresholds of 1.9 / 2.1 mm give 9,095 / 8,072, so 2.0 mm is\n   242→clearly the intended boundary.\n   243→\n   244→---\n   245→\n   246→## Step 5: Mapping Planning\n   247→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   248→\n   249→### Trial window and binning\n   250→- Alignment event: **go cue onset** = `BehavioralEvents/go_start_times.timestamps[trial]`.\n   251→- Window: `off_start = -2.5 s`, `off_end = +1.5 s` → 4.0 s.\n   252→- 50 ms non-overlapping bins → **80 time points**; bin *k* covers `[-2.5 + 0.05k, -2.45 + 0.05k)`\n   253→  relative to the go cue; bin centre = `-2.475 + 0.05k`.\n   254→\n   255→### Variable Mapping\n   256→| Source (NWB) | Target field | Transform | Reference analogue |\n   257→|---|---|---|---|\n   258→| `units/spike_times` (ragged, absolute) − go cue | `neural[s][t]` (n_neurons, 80), float32 | histogram into 80 × 50 ms bins, divide by 0.05 → Hz | `sliding_histogram(..., rate=True)` |\n   259→| `units/classification == 'good'` | neuron mask | keep only `good` | classifier QC lists |\n   260→| `units/anno_name` → Allen ontology → coarse area (+ ALM by AP > 2.0 mm) | `brain_region_idx[s]` | 14 coarse areas | `helper_get_neuron_id_area` |\n   261→| `general/subject/subject_id` | `subjects`, `subject_idx` | one entry per mouse | — |\n   262→| last `sample_start_times` before the go cue | `input[s][t][0]` — `time_from_tone_onset` (s) | `bin_centre − tone_onset`, continuous | `task_sample_time` |\n   263→| `trials/photostim_onset` (+ `start_time`) and `photostim_duration` | `input[s][t][1]` — `photostim` | 1 if the bin centre lies in `[onset, onset+duration)`, else 0 | `task_stimulation[:, 2:4]` |\n   264→| `trials/outcome` + `trials/trial_instruction` | `output[s][t][0]` — `choice` | `ignore`→`no lick`(2); `hit`→instruction; `miss`→opposite instruction | `behavior_report` + `task_trial_type` |\n   265→| `trials/outcome` | `output[s][t][1]` — `outcome` | `ignore`→0, `miss`→1, `hit`→2 | `behavior_report` |\n   266→| `trials/early_lick` | `output[s][t][2]` — `early_lick` | `no early`→0, `early`→1 | `behavior_early_report` |\n   267→| `Camera0_side_TongueTracking` (y, likelihood) | `output[s][t][3]` — `tongue_y` | see below | `align_markers.py` (`tongue_y`) |\n   268→\n   269→All inputs and outputs are **time-varying**, shape (2, 80) and (4, 80). Per-trial variables\n   270→(choice, outcome, early lick) are held constant across the 80 bins, as allowed and encouraged by the\n   271→target format (\"If at all possible, make it time-varying\").\n   272→\n   273→### Tongue y discretisation (per the Decoder Task)\n   274→1. A video frame counts as *tongue visible* if `tongue_likelihood > 0.9` (DLC default cut-off; the\n   275→   likelihood is strongly bimodal so this is not a sensitive choice).\n   276→2. For each 50 ms bin, take the frames whose timestamps fall in the bin. If at least one is visible,\n   277→   the bin's y = mean y over the **visible** frames; otherwise the bin is *not visible* → class 3.\n   278→   (Tongue protrusions last ~50–100 ms, so requiring a majority of the ~15 frames per bin would\n   279→   discard the onset and offset of most licks.)\n   280→3. Per-session 40th and 60th percentiles are computed over **all visible bins of that session**\n   281→   (this is the \"y-position over the session\"), then\n   282→   `0: y < p40`, `1: p40 ≤ y ≤ p60`, `2: y > p60`, `3: not visible`.\n   283→\n   284→### Key Decisions\n   285→1. **Neuron curation = `classification == 'good'`.** Validated by reproducing the paper's per-area\n   286→   unit counts exactly (Step 4 table). No further QC-metric thresholds are applied, because the\n   287→   classifier already consumes all 15 metrics (white paper / `methods.txt`).\n   288→2. **`units/is_good_trials`**: only 0.14 % of (unit, trial) pairs and 565 / 69,453 good units are\n   289→   affected. The target format requires a rectangular (n_neurons, 80) array per trial, so per-trial\n   290→   unit exclusion is impossible; we keep all good units and note the negligible contamination.\n   291→3. **Session curation = the data paper's criteria**: control-trial performance > 65 % and ≥ 50\n   292→   correct lick-left and ≥ 50 correct lick-right trials → **151 of 174 sessions, 28 mice**. This\n   293→   reproduces the paper's trial and performance statistics exactly (Step 4).\n   294→4. **Trial curation**: we keep *all* trials of the selected sessions **except** auto-water and\n   295→   free-water trials (3.7 %). Rationale: on those trials the animal is given water irrespective of\n   296→   its action, so `outcome`/`choice` do not reflect a decision — the reference excludes them in\n   297→   `get_regular_trial_mask`. Early-lick, no-response (ignore) and photostimulation trials are\n   298→   *deliberately kept*, because the Decoder Task requires early lick and \"ignore\"/\"no lick\" as output\n   299→   classes and photostimulation as an input; excluding them would make those classes empty.\n   300→5. **Spikes are sliced in continuous session time**, not clipped at NWB `start_time`/`stop_time`.\n   301→   The reference does the same (its ±3 s window exceeds the trial duration). 15.6 % of trials stop\n   302→   less than 1.5 s after the go cue, so clipping would inject artefactual zero firing rates.\n   303→6. **Video frames are likewise taken by absolute session time.** The camera pauses for ~0.5 s\n   304→   between trials, so a few bins genuinely have no frames; those become class 3 (\"not visible\"),\n   305→   which is the only available representation of missing tracking.\n   306→7. **Firing rates in Hz** (count / 0.05 s), float32 — matches `sliding_histogram(rate=True)`.\n   307→8. **Brain regions**: the 14 coarse areas of datapaper Fig. 1J\n   308→   (`ALM, OtherCortex, Orbital, Olfactory, CorticalSubplate, Hippocampus, Striatum, Pallidum,\n   309→   Thalamus, Hypothalamus, Midbrain, Pons, Medulla, Cerebellum`). Hemisphere is *not* split\n   310→   (the paper's summary statistics are not hemisphere-split either).\n   311→\n   312→### Planned Sanity Checks\n   313→- [x] Good-unit counts per coarse area == datapaper Fig. 1J (done in Step 4).\n   314→- [x] Session selection reproduces \"476 trials (130–785), 84 % correct (65–99 %)\".\n   315→- [x] Median tone onset == −1.85 s re. go cue in every session.\n   316→- [x] Photostim trial count == photostim event count in every session; all stimulation ends at or\n   317→      before the go cue.\n   318→- [x] Choice derived from `outcome`+`trial_instruction` agrees with the first lick after the go cue\n   319→      (99.3–100 % over spot-checked sessions).\n   320→- [x] Tongue visibility is ≈ 0 before the go cue and jumps to ~0.4 immediately after it → video and\n   321→      ephys clocks agree.\n   322→- [ ] Spot-check spike counts, input and output values against the raw NWB (Step 10, Check 2).\n   323→- [ ] Population PSTH aligned to go cue shows the expected response-epoch increase.\n   324→\n   325→---\n   326→\n   327→## Step 6: Script Development\n   328→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   329→\n   330→`/app/convert_data.py`. Run as\n   331→`python -u /app/convert_data.py <outpickle> [--full|--sample] [--show-processing] [--njobs N]`.\n   332→\n   333→Structure:\n   334→\n   335→| Function | Purpose |\n   336→|---|---|\n   337→| `load_ontology`, `assign_brain_regions` | Allen CCF structure graph → 14 coarse areas + ALM carve-out |\n   338→| `read_trial_table` | trials table, go cue times, tone (sample-epoch) onsets |\n   339→| `session_performance`, `session_passes` | data-paper session inclusion criteria (+ ≥1 good unit, ≥2 usable trials) |\n   340→| `observed_trial_mask`, `trial_mask` | trials with ephys coverage, minus auto-/free-water trials |\n   341→| `load_good_units` | `classification == 'good'` units, their spike trains, CCF coordinates and regions |\n   342→| `bin_spikes` | 80 × 50 ms bins aligned to the go cue → Hz |\n   343→| `build_inputs` | time-from-tone-onset ramp and photostim indicator |\n   344→| `choice_codes`, `bin_tongue`, `discretize_tongue`, `build_outputs` | the four output variables |\n   345→| `plot_processing` | the `--show-processing` diagnostic figure |\n   346→| `process_session`, `main` | per-session driver and multiprocessing pool |\n   347→\n   348→**Efficiency**: binning is fully vectorised — for each unit one `np.searchsorted` of the\n   349→`ntrials × 81` bin edges into its sorted spike train, then `np.diff`.  The tongue loop is\n   350→vectorised per trial with `np.bincount`.  Sessions are processed in parallel with a\n   351→`multiprocessing` pool (`--njobs`, default 16).\n   352→\n   353→Code inefficiencies identified and removed:\n   354→- a naive per-(unit, trial, bin) `np.sum(mask)` (the reference `sliding_histogram`\n   355→  pattern) would be ~10^4 times slower; replaced by the searchsorted/diff formulation.\n   356→- per-trial firing-rate matrices are produced by slicing one preallocated\n   357→  `(ntrials, nneurons, 80)` block rather than by concatenating per-trial temporaries.\n   358→\n   359→Measured: **0.2–2 s per session**, 22 s wall clock for all 150 sessions with 24 workers.\n   360→\n   361→---\n   362→\n   363→## Step 7: Sample Conversion and Validation\n   364→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   365→\n   366→`python -u /app/convert_data.py /app/sample_data.pkl --sample --show-processing`\n   367→→ `/app/conversion_sample_out.txt`, `/app/sample_data.pkl`,\n   368→`processing_SC015_20190208_133600_s2.png`, `processing_SC015_20190209_150135_s3.png`.\n   369→\n   370→### Sample Statistics (2 sessions of mouse SC015)\n   371→| Statistic | Value |\n   372→|-----------|-------|\n   373→| Sessions | 2 |\n   374→| Subjects | 1 (SC015) |\n   375→| Neurons (total) | 580 |\n   376→| Neurons / session | 375, 205 |\n   377→| Trials (total) | 676 |\n   378→| Trials / session | 159, 517 |\n   379→| Timepoints / trial | 80 (all trials) |\n   380→| `time_from_tone_onset` range | [-0.63, 5.48] s |\n   381→| `photostim` range | [0, 1], on in 3.2 % of all bins |\n   382→| `choice` distribution | [0.516, 0.462, 0.022] (left, right, no lick) |\n   383→| `outcome` distribution | [0.022, 0.084, 0.893] (ignore, miss, hit) |\n   384→| `early_lick` distribution | [0.978, 0.022] |\n   385→| `tongue_y` distribution | [0.103, 0.052, 0.103, 0.742] |\n   386→\n   387→The tongue fractions confirm the discretisation: of the 25.8 % visible bins, exactly\n   388→40 % / 20 % / 40 % fall into classes 0 / 1 / 2.\n   389→\n   390→### Processing Plots Review\n   391→The figure shows, per session: raw spike ticks against the binned rate (and an\n   392→independent `np.histogram` re-binning with max |difference| = 0); the population PSTH\n   393→(flat before, sharp rise right after the go cue); a z-scored neuron × time map;\n   394→the tone-onset ramp (zero crossing at −1.85 s); the photostim raster (always ending at\n   395→or before the go cue) cross-checked against the raw `photostim_start_times`; the raw\n   396→300 Hz tongue trace with the per-session percentile lines and the resulting class digits;\n   397→P(tongue visible) overlaid on the lick rate (both rise exactly at the go cue); the output\n   398→class fractions; the choice-versus-first-lick confusion matrix (agreement 1.000 in both\n   399→sessions); the per-region neuron counts; and three final `neural[trial]` matrices.\n   400→\n   401→**Anomalies found and fixed during this review** (see also Step 10):\n   402→1. *All-zero neural matrices for 321 of 480 trials in session SC015_…_s2.* Cause: in 9 of\n   403→   the 174 sessions the electrophysiology only covers a contiguous block of the\n   404→   behavioral session. Fixed by masking trials with `units/obs_intervals`.\n   405→2. *One remaining all-zero trial* — the last trial listed in `obs_intervals` extended past\n   406→   the end of the recording. Fixed by dropping any trial in which not one neuron fires.\n   407→3. *Misleading photostim cross-check panel* — the raw events were being assigned to the\n   408→   preceding trial's go cue. Plot-only bug, fixed.\n   409→\n   410→### Run Time Estimates\n   411→| Speed-ups implemented | Time savings |\n   412→|---|---|\n   413→| vectorised searchsorted binning instead of per-bin masks | ~10^3–10^4× on the binning step |\n   414→| single preallocated `(ntrials, nneurons, 80)` block | avoids a second full copy |\n   415→| `multiprocessing` pool over sessions (24 workers) | ~12× wall clock |\n   416→\n   417→| Step | Time / session | Estimated total (150 sessions) |\n   418→|---|---|---|\n   419→| trials + go cues | 0.05 s | 8 s |\n   420→| unit loading (spike times) | 0.06–0.6 s | 30 s |\n   421→| spike binning | 0.1–0.8 s | 60 s |\n   422→| inputs + outputs (incl. video) | 0.16–0.5 s | 40 s |\n   423→| **total, serial** | **0.2–2 s** | **~2.5 min** |\n   424→| **total, 24 workers** | — | **22 s** (measured) |\n   425→\n   426→Well under the 15-minute budget, so no further optimisation was needed.\n   427→\n   428→### Verification\n   429→`python -u /app/train_decoder.py /app/sample_data.pkl --verify-only`\n   430→→ `/app/verification_sample_out.txt`: **\"Data format is valid, no errors or warnings.\"**\n   431→All trials have T = 80; input and output ranges as listed above.\n   432→\n   433→---\n   434→\n   435→## Step 8: Sample Decoder Training\n   436→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   437→\n   438→`python -u /app/train_decoder.py /app/sample_data.pkl` → `/app/train_decoder_sample_out.txt`.\n   439→\n   440→### Format Validation\n   441→- Errors: None\n   442→- Warnings: None\n   443→\n   444→### Training\n   445→Loss decreased monotonically from ~9.2 (epoch 4) to 0.52 (epoch 200); Run over 8 randomly chosen sessions (seed 0), 5 random spot checks each:\n   535→\n   536→| Check | What is compared | Result |\n   537→|---|---|---|\n   538→| Trial correspondence | rebuilt keep-mask 551→- `read_trial_table` raises if the number of go cues differs from the number of trials.\n   552→- `observed_trial_mask` raises if `obs_intervals` differ between the first and the last\n   553→  unit of a session, or if they do not line up exactly with the trial table.\n   554→- `main` asserts that mouse name ↔ DANDI subject id is a 1:1 mapping.\n   555→- the `--show-processing` figure re-bins one trial with `np.histogram` (max |diff| = 0)\n   556→  and re-derives choice from the raw lick event times (agreement 1.000).\n   557→\n   558→### Check 3 — reference code comparison\n   559→| Stage | Reference (`preprocessing_DJ_2022Aug.py`, `align_markers.py`, `population_decoding_utils.py`) | This conversion | Same? |\n   560→|---|---|---|---|\n   561→| (a) Loading | `loadmat` of DataJoint `.mat` exports, probes concatenated per session | `h5py` on the published NWB; NWB stores all probes of a session in one `units` table, so no concatenation is needed | equivalent |\n   562→| (b) Neuron filtering | units on the classifier \"good unit\" list, with a CCF annotation, hemisphere split at ML 5700 µm | `units/classification == 'good'` (all such units have an annotation); hemisphere not split | equivalent; hemisphere pooling is a deliberate choice (papers' summary statistics are not hemisphere-split, and the format asks for region names such as \"ALM\") |\n   563→| (c) Alignment | spike times relative to `task_cue_time` (go cue); markers aligned to the go cue | spike/video times minus `go_start_times` | same |\n   564→| (d) Binning | `sliding_histogram`, `bw = 0.04 s`, `stride = 0.0034 s`, rate = counts/bw | 50 ms width, 50 ms stride, rate = counts/0.05 s | **differs by task mandate** (Decoder Task requires 50 ms bins); the rate convention is identical |\n   565→| (e) Window | −3 … +3 s (ephys) / −3 … +1.5 s (markers) re. go cue | −2.5 … +1.5 s | **differs by task mandate** |\n   566→| (f) Trial filtering | `get_regular_trial_mask`: no early lick, no auto-water, no free-water, response given, no photostim | auto-/free-water excluded; early-lick, no-response and photostim trials **kept** | **differs by task mandate** — those three are decoder outputs/inputs; excluding them would leave the `no lick`, `ignore` and `early = yes` classes empty |\n   567→| Session filtering | data paper: performance > 65 %, ≥ 50 correct per direction | identical | same |\n   568→| Trial coverage | implicit (the export only contains recorded trials) | explicit via `units/obs_intervals` | equivalent |\n   569→| (g) Inputs | reference uses task variables only as labels | `time_from_tone_onset` from `task_sample_time`/`sample_start_times`; `photostim` from `task_stimulation`/`photostim_onset`+`duration` | same source variables |\n   570→| (h) Tongue | `align_markers.py` takes the last frame in each 3.4 ms window; occluded tongue imputed with the session mean | mean of the *visible* frames in each 50 ms bin; occlusion is its own class | **differs by task mandate** — the Decoder Task defines \"not visible\" as a 4th class, which is incompatible with mean-imputation |\n   571→\n   572→### Check 4 — key statistics comparison\n   573→See the table in Step 9. Every per-area unit count of datapaper Fig. 1J is reproduced\n   574→**exactly** on the full 174-session dataset (thalamus 12,808; orbital 10,223; striatum\n   575→7,664; midbrain 7,495; olfactory 4,137; medulla 2,928; cortical subplate 1,960;\n   576→hippocampus 1,944; cerebellum 1,820; pallidum 1,092; hypothalamus 815; pons 347), ALM to\n   577→within 0.1 % (8,728 vs 8,717), and the behavioral statistics (\"476 trials, range\n   578→130–785\", \"84 % correct, range 65–99 %\") are reproduced to 478.6 / 130–785 and\n   579→83.7 % / 65.8–98.9 %. 5.2 % of trials then have their tone onset before the window start, which simply makes `time_from_tone_onset` start above 0 |\n   592→| Trial with no preceding `sample_start` | falls back to the nominal go cue − 1.85 s (never triggered: 0 of 94,990 trials) |\n   593→| Bin-edge convention | half-open `[edge_k, edge_{k+1})` everywhere, identical to the reference's `(t >= lo) & (t < hi)`; verified against `np.histogram` |\n   594→| Photostim strings `'N/A'` | treated as \"no stimulation\"; the trials-table count equals the `photostim_start_times` event count in all 174 sessions |\n   595→| Session with no visible tongue at all | `discretize_tongue` returns all-3 instead of failing (never triggered) |\n   596→| `units/is_good_trials` False | affects 0.14 % of (unit, trial) pairs and 565/69,453 units; not actionable because the format requires a rectangular `(n_neurons, 80)` matrix per trial. Documented, not fixed. |\n   597→\n   598→### Iterations\n   599→1. First sample run → all-zero neural matrices → added the `obs_intervals` mask → re-ran\n   600→   sample conversion, verification, plots. Re-ran all checks.\n   601→2. This is the expected signature: `choice`, `outcome` and `early_lick` are\n   635→properties of the whole trial but can only be read out of the neural activity once the\n   636→information is present in the brain, so pooling all 80 bins necessarily caps the\n   637→attainable balanced accuracy.\n   638→\n   639→---\n   640→\n   641→## Step 12: Critical Review 2\n   642→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   643→\n   644→### Check 1 — accuracy vs chance\n   645→| Output | Validation balanced acc. | Chance | Ratio | Verdict |\n   646→|---|---|---|---|---|\n   647→| choice | 0.6766 | 0.3333 | 2.03× | ✔ |\n   648→| outcome | 0.6481 | 0.3333 | 1.94× | ✔ |\n   649→| early_lick | 0.7479 | 0.5000 | 1.50× | at the 1.5× line — investigated below |\n   650→| tongue_y | 0.6597 | 0.2500 | 2.64× | ✔ |\n   651→\n   652→Nothing is below chance. `early_lick` is intrinsically the hardest of the four, and its\n   653→ceiling is structural rather than a conversion defect:\n   654→- an \"early lick\" is a lick anywhere in the sample **or** delay epoch, and an early lick\n   655→  triggers a *replay* of that epoch. The neural correlate therefore often falls **before**\n   656→  the −2.5 s window boundary (5.2 % of trials have their tone onset before the window\n   657→  starts, and those are exactly the replayed, i.e. early-lick, trials). Widening the\n   658→  window is not possible — it is fixed by the Decoder Task.\n   659→- for a binary variable the ratio to chance is bounded by 2.0, so 1.50× corresponds to\n   660→  the same \"distance from chance\" as 2.0× would for a 4-class variable.\n   661→\n   662→### Check 2 — accuracy comparison to the papers\n   663→No paper reports a neural decoder for outcome, early lick or tongue position, so the only\n   664→directly comparable quantity is **choice**. The method paper reports choice decoding from\n   665→*video* (embedding method, logistic regression, AUC of ROC), per epoch. To compare\n   666→like-with-like I reproduced that analysis on the converted **neural** data\n   667→(`cache/time_resolved_decoding.py`: PCA-16 + logistic regression, 5-fold stratified CV,\n   668→independently at each of the 80 bins; 6 randomly chosen sessions, left vs right trials):\n   669→\n   670→| Epoch | Method paper (video → choice) | This conversion (neural → choice) |\n   671→|---|---|---|\n   672→| Pre-sample (t < −1.85 s) | AUC 0.51 ± 0.06 s.d. | **0.538 ± 0.035** |\n   673→| 2nd half of sample + delay (−1.5 … 0 s) | AUC 0.66 ± 0.12 s.d. | **0.752 ± 0.062** |\n   674→| 2nd half of the response epoch (t > 0.75 s) | AUC 0.99 ± 0.01 s.d. | **0.910 ± 0.032** |\n   675→\n   676→Interpretation: the pre-sample value sits at chance, exactly as the paper requires — this\n   677→is a strong alignment check, because any leakage of trial identity into the pre-stimulus\n   678→period (e.g. an off-by-one trial shift) would push it far above 0.5. The delay-epoch value\n   679→is *higher* than the paper's video-based number, as expected, since the recordings include\n   680→ALM, whose delay activity is the canonical choice-selective signal of this dataset. The\n   681→response-epoch value (0.91) is below the video's 0.99, which is also expected: the video\n   682→literally shows the tongue moving left or right, whereas single 50 ms bins of ~400 neurons\n   683→are a noisier read-out, and (unlike the paper) we include photostimulation, early-lick and\n   684→error trials in this analysis. The saved curve is `cache/choice_auc_vs_time.png`.\n   685→\n   686→### Check 3 — train vs validation gap\n   687→| Output | Train | Validation | Train / Val |\n   688→|---|---|---|---|\n   689→| choice | 0.7208 | 0.6766 | 1.065 |\n   690→| outcome | 0.7077 | 0.6481 | 1.092 |\n   691→| early_lick | 0.7843 | 0.7479 | 1.049 |\n   692→| tongue_y | 0.6841 | 0.6597 | 1.037 |\n   693→\n   694→All far below the 1.5× threshold — no overfitting and no sign of leakage (a leak would\n   695→show up as a *high* validation accuracy, and specifically as an above-chance pre-stimulus\n   696→AUC in Check 2, which it does not).\n   697→\n   698→### Further debugging steps performed\n   699→1. **Output values verified on raw data** — `cache/sanity_checks.py`, 8 sessions × 5\n   700→   random trials per output, plus a full trial × bin comparison of the tongue classes.\n   701→   0 failures (Step 10, Check 2).\n   702→2. **Temporal alignment verified** — three independent checks, all confirming that the\n   703→   video, lick-event, photostim and spike clocks agree with the go cue:\n   704→   P(tongue visible) rises from 0.074 to 0.541 exactly at t = 0 and tracks the lick rate\n   705→   bin for bin; none\n   709→   is degenerate, and `train_decoder.py` runs with `balanced_loss=True`.\n   710→4. **Neuron filtering re-checked** against the paper's per-area unit counts — exact match\n   711→   on 12 of 14 areas (Step 10, Check 4).\n   712→5. **Processing re-checked** against the reference code, step by step (Step 10, Check 3).\n   713→\n   714→### Investigated and deliberately not adopted: rescaling the neural array\n   715→The provided decoder is a linear projection (SVD-initialised) plus a linear read-out,\n   716→trained with a fixed learning rate and L1 penalty, and it never standardises the neural\n   717→input — so its accuracy depends on the *units* of the neural array even though the\n   718→information content does not. Sweeping a global scale factor on a 12-session subset\n   719→(`cache/scale_sweep.py`, `cache/scale_test.py`):\n   720→\n   721→| Neural array | choice | outcome | early_lick | tongue_y |\n   722→|---|---|---|---|---|\n   723→| firing rate in Hz (**shipped**) | 0.7015 | 0.6623 | 0.7314 | 0.6600 |\n   724→| × 0.2 | 0.7191 | 0.6771 | 0.7373 | 0.6833 |\n   725→| × 0.05 (= spike count per 50 ms bin) | 0.7255 | 0.6794 | 0.7408 | 0.6932 |\n   726→| × 0.01 (arbitrary unit) | 0.7273 | 0.6846 | 0.7439 | 0.6964 |\n   727→| per-neuron z-score | 0.7156 | 0.6394 | 0.7227 | 0.6760 |\n   728→\n   729→Smaller units help by 1–3 points, and the gain saturates. **We ship firing rates in Hz**\n   730→anyway, because (a) that is exactly what the reference pipeline produces\n   731→(`sliding_histogram(..., rate=True)` = counts / bin width) and the task requires matching\n   732→the reference's processing, (b) Hz is the standard, interpretable unit and is documented\n   733→in `metadata['neural_units']`, and (c) the 1–3 point difference is an artefact of one\n   734→decoder's optimiser settings, not a property of the data — rescaling by an arbitrary\n   735→constant to suit a particular optimiser would be tuning the dataset to the metric. The\n   736→measurement is reported here so that the choice is explicit and reversible (multiply\n   737→`neural` by 0.05 to obtain spike counts).\n   738→\n   739→### Issues found and resolved relative to go cue: sample `[-1.9, -1.2]`, delay `[-1.2, 0.0]`, post-go `[0.0, 1.0]` |\n\n### Notes\n- The reference pipeline consumes **DataJoint `.mat` exports**, not the published NWB files. Every\n  field it uses has a direct NWB counterpart (table below in Step 4), so the logic transfers.\n- **Time base**: in the `.mat` export the per-trial spike times are already expressed relative to\n  the go cue. In NWB, `units/spike_times` are absolute session times, so we subtract the trial's go\n  cue time ourselves. This is the same alignment.\n- The reference truncation window (`-3 s` … `+3/3.5 s` around the go cue) is *wider than a typical\n  trial* (median trial start is 3.15 s before, and median trial stop 1.8 s after the go cue),\n  confirming that per-trial spike trains in the export are not clipped at NWB `start_time`/\n  `stop_time`. We therefore slice spikes by absolute session time, which reproduces this.\n- Neuron curation in the reference = units on the classifier-based \"good unit\" list **and** having a\n  CCF annotation. In NWB this is `units/classification == 'good'` (`units/anno_name` is then always\n  non-empty — verified: 0 of 69,453 good units lack an annotation).\n- `get_regular_trial_mask` is the *analysis* mask used for the figures of the method paper. It cannot\n  be applied here: the decoder task explicitly requires photostimulation as an input and\n  early-lick / no-response (ignore) trials as outputs. See Step 5 for what we do keep.\n\n---\n\n## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Data `acquisition/BehavioralTimeSeries/` | `Camera0_side_{Jaw,Nose,Tongue}Tracking`, each `data` = (n_frames, 3) = (x, y, DLC likelihood), `timestamps` = session time, 300 Hz (dt = 0.0034 s) |\n| `units/` | `spike_times` + `spike_times_index` (ragged, absolute session time), `classification` (`good`/`unlabelled`), `anno_name` (CCF structure name), `unit_quality`, `is_good_trials` (n_units × n_trials), 15 QC metrics, `electrodes` (index into electrode table) |\n| `general/extracellular_ephys/electrodes` | `x`, `y`, `z` = CCF coordinates (ML, DV, AP) in µm, `location` = JSON with the targeted `brain_regions` (e.g. `\"left ALM\"`) |\n\n### Dataset Size (from data files, all 174 sessions)\n| Statistic | Value |\n|-----------|-------|\n| NWB files / sessions | 174 |\n| Subjects (mice) | 28 |\n| Sessions / subject | 1–11 (mean 6.2) |\n| Units (all clusters) | 272,227 |\n| Units `classification == 'good'` | **69,453** (25.5 % of all clusters) |\n| Good units / session | mean 399, range |\n\n### Processing Details\n- **Temporal alignment**: everything is aligned to the **go cue** (`task_cue_time` in the export;\n  `go_start_times` in NWB). The reference marker alignment also uses the go cue with a window of\n  −3 … +1.5 s. Our task window is −2.5 … +1.5 s.\n- **Temporal binning**: reference 40 ms / 3.4 ms sliding; the Decoder Task here mandates **50 ms\n  bins** → 80 non-overlapping bins over the 4 s window. Firing rate = spike count / 0.05 s (Hz),\n  exactly the reference's `sliding_histogram(..., rate=True)` convention.\n- **Trial structure**: presample → sample (0.65 s) → delay (1.2 s) → go cue → response (1.5 s) →\n  trial end. Licking during sample/delay triggers a *replay* of the epoch, so a trial can have more\n  than one `sample_start_times` / `delay_start_times` event (5.8 % of trials).\n\n### Curation Steps\n\n**Neuron curation rules (reference)**:\n1. Unit must be labelled `good` by the region-specific logistic-regression QC classifier\n   (`classification == 'good'`; this is the \"8717 from ALM …\" set).\n2. Unit must have a CCF histology annotation (`anno_name` non-empty) — automatically satisfied.\n3. (Method paper, analysis-level only) brain areas with < 10 neurons in a session were dropped from\n   *per-area* analyses; not applicable here because we pool all neurons of a session.\n\n**Trial curation rules (reference)**:\n- `get_regular_trial_mask`: no early lick, no auto-water, no free-water, response given, no\n  photostimulation — used for the figure analyses.\n- Data paper: \"Early lick trials and no response trials were excluded for analysis.\"\n\n**Session curation rules (reference)**: performance > 65 % and ≥ 50 correct lick-left and\nlick-right trials.\n\n### Decoders Trained (reference)\n| Decoded variable | Method | Accuracy |\n|---|---|---|\n| Trial type / choice from **video** (pre-sample) | embedding + logistic regression | AUC 0.51 ± 0.06 (chance) |\n| Trial type / choice from **video** (2nd half sample + delay) | same | AUC 0.66 ± 0.12 |\n| Trial type / choice from **video** (2nd half of response epoch) | same | AUC 0.99 ± 0.01 |\n| Choice from **neural activity** (medulla population, delay) | PCA + logistic regression | reported per-session AUC, Fig. 7 |\n\nNo paper reports a neural decoder for outcome, early lick or tongue position, so the only\nquantitative expectation is that **choice decoding after the go cue should be near-perfect**\n(video reaches AUC 0.99; neural recordings include ALM, which is strongly choice-selective).\n\n---\n\n## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### NWB ↔ DataJoint-export field mapping (reference code → NWB)\n| Reference `.mat` field | NWB equivalent | |\n|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n| Number of sessions | – | 174 NWB files | 173 behavioral sessions | The published dandiset has one extra file; all statistics below are computed on the 174 files and still match the paper. Not an error on our side. |\n| Total good units | – | 69,453 | 69,943 | 0.7 % fewer. The entire difference (490) is in cortex (see per-area table below); every subcortical area matches **exactly**. Different dandiset version; nothing to fix. |\n| Trials/session | – | mean 546 (all trials), mean 465 (excl. no-response) | mean 476, range 130–785 | Resolved: the paper's number counts trials **excluding no-response trials** and only over sessions passing the > 65 % performance criterion.
